Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on composites structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 227. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 522 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_227(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 227. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^522(S, F) is trivial. QED.
